Context\n\n### Key Decisions\n\nSee PROJECT.md Key Decisions table for full list with outcomes.\n\nRecent for v1.1:\n\n- Network-first with self-hosted Someguy + DB fallback adopted as IPNS resolution strategy (revised from DB-first during Phase 19 context -- see 19-SCOPING_RATIONALE.md #1)\n- rootFolderKey DB copy kept as permanent fallback (never drop column, IPFS copy for recovery independence)\n- BYO-IPFS affects pinning only, all IPNS publishes still route through CipherBox API\n- PERF requirements split across Phase 18 (server-side, pre-change) and Phase 22 (client + load testing, post-change)\n- IPFS/IPNS histogram buckets: 1ms-30s exponential (14 buckets); republish batch: 1s-120s (10 buckets)\n- Source label (db/network) only for resolve operations; empty string for pin/cat/publish\n- Alloy scrapes Kubo directly via Docker internal network (ipfs:5001), not proxied through API\n- Kubo Health dashboard panels use fallback Go runtime metrics alongside libp2p metrics pending post-deploy verification\n- IPNS-specific histograms: resolve 50ms-30s, publish 100ms-60s with source/outcome labels\n- Null resolve results (not found) excluded from IPNS histogram observations\n- Used axios-functions orval client for @cipherbox/api-client (plain functions, no React deps)\n- sdk-core IPFS ops use direct axios/fetch (not api-client) for upload progress; IPNS ops use api-client generated functions\n- Bin/share operations take explicit context objects (BinOperationContext, ShareOperationContext) instead of Zustand stores\n- Share module accepts callback functions for API calls to stay transport-decoupled\n- Moved @cipherbox/core from dependencies to devDependencies in crypto (test-only cross-package assertions)\n- Kubo pebbleds datastore (LSM-tree) configured via IPFS_PROFILE=server,pebbleds; requires fresh volume on deploy\n- SDK concurrent pins require pebbleds datastore (synergistic); concurrent pins alone cause regression at 50 clients\n- Combined per-task commits into single commit due to pre-commit hook requiring api-client regeneration with entity/dto/controller changes\n- Desktop root folder detected by inode::ROOT_INO at publish call sites (simpler than modifying build_folder_metadata return type)\n- Desktop initialize_vault produces v2 blob for new users from day one (not just on migration)\n- decrypt_metadata_from_ipfs_public transparently handles both v1 JSON and v2 binary blobs\n- VaultExportDto returns only rootIpnsName and derivationMethod (crypto columns dropped)\n- Recovery tool IPNS resolution uses gateway /ipns/ HEAD request with redirect following (most reliable without API dependency)\n- fetchAndDecryptMetadata handles both v1 JSON and v2 binary blobs transparently for folder sync\n- Zero-crypto vault schema: server stores only ownerPublicKey and rootIpnsName, all crypto material lives exclusively in IPFS v2 blobs\n- DB crypto columns (encrypted_root_folder_key, encrypted_root_ipns_private_key, migrated_at) fully dropped — no fallback paths\n- PinningProvider interface: KuboProvider uses Basic auth, PsaProvider uses Bearer auth, matching each protocol's native auth model\n- PsaProvider.pin() throws intentionally; pinByCid() is the correct PSA workflow (CID-reference-only protocol)\n- Connection test uses sequential probe: Kubo /api/v0/id first, then PSA /pins, with 10s timeout per probe\n- CID registration gated to BYO users only via ForbiddenException (non-BYO users cannot bypass upload relay)\n- Advisory quota: checkQuota() always true for BYO, getQuota() includes advisory boolean flag for UI display\n- pinFn injection pattern: optional pinFn parameter on sdkCore.uploadFile() replaces addToIpfs when BYO mode active\n- External+Kubo bypasses CipherBox entirely; external+PSA uses relay for CID only; dual does both with best-effort secondary\n- PsaProvider.pinByCid() accessed via cast in client.ts (PSA-specific, not on PinningProvider interface)\n- Migration uses existing BullMQ pattern with pin-migration queue name; TEE decrypts ECIES-encrypted provider configs in-enclave with epoch key\n- SSRF protection on TEE migration: validates URL structure (HTTPS-only, no private IPs) and DNS resolution (rebinding check)\n- BYO config stored as encrypted IPNS entry using rootFolderKey — no server-side credential storage (zero-knowledge preserved)\n- Dedicated IPNS key derived via HKDF with context string byo-ipfs-config from vault keypair\n- BYO benchmark execution (21-07 Task 4) deferred — requires external IPFS provider infrastructure; test scenarios ready to run when provider available\n- BYO config loaded at login via IPNS resolve with graceful fallback to cipherbox-only mode\n- Source unpin is best-effort and non-fatal after verified CID transfer to destination\n- Cargo workspace with centralized deps at repo root; cipherbox-crypto crate as foundation for all Rust SDK extraction\n- Module re-export pattern in desktop crypto/mod.rs preserves all existing crate::crypto::\\* paths without touching call sites\n- cipherbox-core crate layered on cipherbox-crypto: folder, file, bin, vault_blob, ipns, registry, decrypt, error modules\n- File module re-exports FileMetadata types from folder.rs (shared AES encryption context with parent folder key)\n- decrypt module moved from fuse to crypto re-export (domain logic, not FUSE-specific)\n- Hand-structured API client crate rather than openapi-generator (modest API surface, proven code, no Java/Docker CI dependency)\n- critical-section std feature required for standalone ecies linking (Tauri provides it in desktop builds)\n- Shared test vectors in tests/vectors/ JSON files loadable by both Rust and TypeScript for CI parity gates\n- SyncDaemon uses Arc<dyn Fn(SyncStatus)> generic callback instead of Tauri AppHandle for testability\n- Desktop api/client.rs re-exports cipherbox_api_client::ApiClient as type alias to unify types across modules\n- Desktop AppState wraps Arc<KeyState> from SDK; all key material accessed via state.sdk.\\*\n- Keychain operations kept as desktop-specific keychain.rs module (not in api-client crate)\n- Desktop api/ and crypto/ directories fully removed; all imports use workspace crates directly\n- CI parity gate uses needs.changes.outputs.src (not nonexistent packages) for trigger condition\n- Desktop-e2e binary paths updated to target/debug/ to match workspace cargo build output\n- PinataProvider uses dual base URLs: uploads.pinata.cloud (fixed) for upload, api.pinata.cloud (configurable) for management\n- pinWithMode treats Pinata like Kubo: direct upload bypasses CipherBox relay entirely\n- Connection test probe order updated: Kubo -> Pinata -> PSA; pinata.cloud URLs skip Kubo probe\n- BYO Pinata baselines: pin p50=2.0s (+47% vs local Kubo), tail latency p99 13.5% better, 98% CipherBox API load reduction per file\n- perf.ts PERF_ENABLED evaluated once at module load (zero overhead in production); **CIPHERBOX_PERF** global for opt-in production debugging\n- Load test thresholds set at 2-3x observed baselines; spike test most generous (15s/15%); vitest expect() for CI failure on breach\n- Write-share authorization in upsertFolderIpns falls through to create-new-entry when no write share found (preserves backward compat for owner first publish)\n- TEE enrollFolder uses existing.userId (FolderIpns owner) for write-share publishes, not authenticated userId\n- Per-file IPNS records created for shared uploads (same as owner uploads) instead of empty fileMetaIpnsName PoC shortcut\n- File IPNS private key dual-wrapped: owner key in FilePointer, recipient key in share_keys (keyType: file-ipns)\n- addShareKeys API relaxed to allow write-share recipients to add keys to their own share\n- TextEditorDialog has separate shared file save path via onSaveSharedFile callback\n- Shared file download/view falls back to fileKeyEncrypted from metadata when no share_key exists\n- FilePointer resolution uses FileMetadata directly (no separate ResolvedFileMetadata struct)\n- FilePointer resolution scoped to parent folder via get_unresolved_file_pointers_for_parent() to avoid wrong-folder-key decryption\n- FilePointer async resolution: 500ms base \\* 2^attempt exponential backoff (1s, 2s, 4s) with 3 retries\n- Removed custom dstack-sdk.d.ts since @phala/dstack-sdk@0.5.7 ships own TypeScript types\n- Defensive CVM key derivation handles both key (v0.5+) and asUint8Array (legacy) SDK return types\n- TEE worker Prometheus metrics use `cipherbox_tee_*` prefix for Grafana dashboard coexistence with API metrics\n- TEE worker structured JSON logger has zero external dependencies (JSON.stringify to stdout/stderr)\n\n### Roadmap Evolution\n\n- Phase 19.1 inserted after Phase 19: Extract core crypto SDK as shared package (URGENT)\n- Phase 19.2 inserted after Phase 19: IPFS Upload Performance shared cipherbox-core crate, replace duplicated logic in desktop FUSE code, enable unit testing parity with TypeScript\n- Phase 27 added: Writable Shares (PoC) — extend read-only sharing to read-write Now let me present the checkpoint for user verification.

## CHECKPOINT REACHED

**Type:** human-verify
**Debug Session:** .planning/debug/fuse-folder-rename.md
**Progress:** 5 evidence entries, 1 hypothesis eliminated

### Investigation State

**Current Hypothesis:** CONFIRMED -- Two separate root causes, both fixed

**Evidence So Far:**

- `access()` already returns OK unconditionally (ruled out as cause)
- Directory permissions `0o755` are too restrictive for FUSE-T SMB backend -- the SMB server layer does its own permission checking based on `getattr` results, and when the SMB client UID doesn't match the FUSE-reported owner, write operations (including rename, which requires write permission on the parent directory) are denied
- `populate_folder()` matches children by display name only via `find_child(parent_ino, &folder.name)` -- when a folder is renamed remotely (same `ipns_name`, new `name`), `find_child` returns `None`, a new inode is allocated, and `merge_only=true` preserves the old inode, creating duplicates

### Fixes Applied

**BUG 1 -- FUSE rename permission failure:**

- Changed all FUSE directory permissions from `0o755` to `0o777` and file permissions from `0o644` to `0o666`
- Encryption is the real access control, not Unix permissions
- Files changed: `crates/fuse/src/inode.rs` (root inode, populate_folder folders/files), `crates/fuse/src/write_ops.rs` (handle_mkdir, handle_create)

**BUG 2 -- Sync creates duplicate folders:**

- Enhanced `populate_folder()` to build `ipns_name -> ino` and `file_meta_ipns_name -> ino` lookup maps from existing children
- When `find_child` by name fails, falls back to matching by stable IPNS identifier
- On IPNS match (rename detected), cleans up old name-to-ino index entry before inserting with new name
- Updated non-merge removal logic to check IPNS names instead of display names, preventing renamed items from being incorrectly removed
- Added 2 new unit tests that specifically verify folder rename matching by IPNS name
- File changed: `crates/fuse/src/inode.rs`

### Self-verified checks

- All 39 unit tests pass (including 2 new rename-specific tests)
- Full desktop app compiles cleanly (`cargo check -p cipherbox-desktop`)
- Windows platform code unaffected (separate feature gate)

### How to check

1. Build and run the desktop app: `pnpm --filter desktop dev`
2. **BUG 1:** Mount FUSE, try renaming a folder in Finder or terminal (`mv OldName NewName` inside `~/CipherBox`). Should work without any Touch ID dialog or permission error.
3. **BUG 2:** Create a folder in the desktop app, then rename it in the web UI, then trigger a sync on the desktop (wait for background IPNS refresh or remount). Should show only the renamed folder, not both old and new.

**Tell me:** "confirmed fixed" OR what's still failing

+    fn generate_test_keypair() -> (Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>) {

+
+    #[test]
+    fn test_populate_folder_matches_renamed_folder_by_ipns_name() {
+        let mut table = InodeTable::new();
+
+        // Generate a real secp256k1 keypair for ECIES operations

+
+        // Create a 32-byte folder key and wrap it with ECIES
+        let folder_key_raw = vec![42u8; 32];
+        let folder_key_encrypted_hex = hex::encode(
+            cipherbox_crypto::ecies::wrap_key(&folder_key_raw, &public_key).unwrap()
+        );
+
+        // Create a 32-byte IPNS private key and wrap it
+        let ipns_key_raw = vec![7u8; 32];
+        let ipns_key_encrypted_hex = hex::encode(
+            cipherbox_crypto::ecies::wrap_key(&ipns_key_raw, &public_key).unwrap()
+        );
+
+        let ipns_name = "REDACTED";
+
+        // Initial population: folder named "OldName"
+        let metadata_v1 = FolderMetadata {
+            version: "v2".to_string(),
+            children: vec![

+                    name: "OldName".to_string(),
+                    ipns_name: ipns_name.to_string(),
+                    folder_key_encrypted: folder_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    ipns_private_key_encrypted: ipns_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    created_at: 1700000000000,
+                    modified_at: 1700000000000,
+                }),
+            ],
+        };
+
+        table.populate_folder(ROOT_INO, &metadata_v1, &private_key, &public_key, false).unwrap();
+
+        let root = table.get(ROOT_INO).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(root.children.as_ref().unwrap().len(), 1);
+        let original_ino = root.children.as_ref().unwrap()[0];
+        let child = table.get(original_ino).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(child.name, "OldName");
+
+        // Now rename folder in remote metadata: same ipns_name, new name
+        let metadata_v2 = FolderMetadata {
+            version: "v2".to_string(),
+            children: vec![
+                FolderChild::Folder(cipherbox_core::folder::FolderEntry {
+                    id: "folder-1".to_string(),
+                    name: "NewName".to_string(),
+                    ipns_name: ipns_name.to_string(),
+                    folder_key_encrypted: folder_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    ipns_private_key_encrypted: ipns_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    created_at: 1700000000000,
+                    modified_at: 1700001000000,
+                }),
+            ],
+        };
+
+        // merge_only=true (background sync)

+

+        // Should have exactly 1 child, not 2 (the old folder should be reused, not duplicated)
+        assert_eq!(
+            root.children.as_ref().unwrap().len(), 1,
+            "Renamed folder should not create duplicate -- expected 1 child, got {}",
+            root.children.as_ref().unwrap().len()
+        );
+
+        // The ino should be reused
+        let child_ino = root.children.as_ref().unwrap()[0];
+        assert_eq!(child_ino, original_ino, "Inode should be reused after rename");
+
+        // Name should be updated
+        let child = table.get(child_ino).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(child.name, "NewName", "Folder name should be updated to new name");
+
+        // Old name should NOT be findable
+        assert!(table.find_child(ROOT_INO, "OldName").is_none(), "Old name should not be findable");
+
+        // New name should be findable
+        assert_eq!(table.find_child(ROOT_INO, "NewName"), Some(original_ino), "New name should map to same ino");
+
+        // IPNS name should be unchanged
+        match &child.kind {
+            InodeKind::Folder { ipns_name: stored_ipns, .. } => {
+                assert_eq!(stored_ipns, ipns_name, "IPNS name should be preserved");
+            }
+            _ => panic!("Expected Folder kind"),
+        }
+    }

+        let mut table = InodeTable::new();

+        let ipns_key_raw = vec![7u8; 32];
+        let ipns_key_encrypted_hex = hex::encode(
+            cipherbox_crypto::ecies::wrap_key(&ipns_key_raw, &public_key).unwrap()
+        );
+
+        let ipns_name = "k51qzi5uqu5dFOLDERstableID";
+
+        // Initial: folder named "Alpha"
+        let metadata_v1 = FolderMetadata {
+            version: "v2".to_string(),
+            children: vec![
+                FolderChild::Folder(cipherbox_core::folder::FolderEntry {
+                    id: "folder-1".to_string(),
+                    name: "Alpha".to_string(),
+                    ipns_name: ipns_name.to_string(),
+                    folder_key_encrypted: folder_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    ipns_private_key_encrypted: ipns_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    created_at: 1700000000000,
+                    modified_at: 1700000000000,
+                }),
+            ],
+        };
+
+        table.populate_folder(ROOT_INO, &metadata_v1, &private_key, &public_key, false).unwrap();
+        let original_ino = table.get(ROOT_INO).unwrap().children.as_ref().unwrap()[0];
+
+        // Non-merge (initial mount) with renamed folder
+        let metadata_v2 = FolderMetadata {
+            version: "v2".to_string(),
+            children: vec![
+                FolderChild::Folder(cipherbox_core::folder::FolderEntry {
+                    id: "folder-1".to_string(),
+                    name: "Beta".to_string(),
+                    ipns_name: ipns_name.to_string(),
+                    folder_key_encrypted: folder_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    ipns_private_key_encrypted: ipns_key_encrypted_hex.clone(),
+                    created_at: 1700000000000,
+                    modified_at: 1700001000000,
+                }),
+            ],
+        };
+
+        // merge_only=false (initial mount / full refresh)
+        table.populate_folder(ROOT_INO, &metadata_v2, &private_key, &public_key, false).unwrap();
+
+        let root = table.get(ROOT_INO).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(root.children.as_ref().unwrap().len(), 1, "Should have exactly 1 child");
+
+        let child_ino = root.children.as_ref().unwrap()[0];
+        assert_eq!(child_ino, original_ino, "Inode should be reused after rename");
+
+        let child = table.get(child_ino).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(child.name, "Beta", "Name should be updated");
+        assert!(table.find_child(ROOT_INO, "Alpha").is_none(), "Old name should not exist");
+        assert_eq!(table.find_child(ROOT_INO, "Beta"), Some(original_ino));
+    }

All 39 tests pass (including 2 new rename-specific tests). Here's the summary:

---

## Root Causes Found & Fixed

### Bug 1: FUSE rename requires elevated permissions

**Root cause:** Directory permissions were set to `0o755` and file permissions to `0o644`. On macOS with FUSE-T's SMB backend, the SMB server layer performs its own permission checking based on `getattr` results. When the SMB client UID doesn't match the FUSE-reported owner, write operations (including rename, which requires write permission on the parent directory) are denied — triggering the Touch ID dialog and ultimately failing.

**Fix:** Changed all directory permissions to `0o777` and file permissions to `0o666`. Encryption is the real access control in CipherBox, not Unix permissions.

**Files:** `crates/fuse/src/inode.rs`, `crates/fuse/src/write_ops.rs`

### Bug 2: Sync creates duplicate folders after web rename

**Root cause:** `populate_folder()` matched children by display **name** only. When a folder is renamed remotely (same `ipns_name`, new `name`), `find_child()` returned `None`, a new inode was allocated, and the `merge_only=true` preservation logic kept the old inode — creating duplicates.

**Fix:** Enhanced `populate_folder()` to build `ipns_name -> ino` lookup maps from existing children. When name-based matching fails, falls back to matching by stable IPNS identifier. On IPNS match (rename detected), cleans up old name index entry. Also updated the non-merge removal logic to check IPNS names instead of display names.

**File:** `crates/fuse/src/inode.rs`

---

**To verify:** Build and run the desktop app, then:
1. Try renaming a folder in Finder — should work without any elevation dialog
2. Rename a folder in web UI, wait for sync — should show only the renamed folder, not both

## Desktop E2E Test Infrastructure Documentation

Based on my thorough exploration of the CipherBox project, here's a complete analysis of the desktop e2e test infrastructure:

---

### 1. **Location of Desktop E2E Tests**

**Primary location:** `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/tests/desktop-e2e/`

**Test script files:**
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/run-all.sh` - Master orchestrator (macOS/Linux)
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/run-all.ps1` - Master orchestrator (Windows)
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-fuse-operations.sh` - FUSE filesystem I/O tests (Bash)
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-fuse-operations.ps1` - FUSE filesystem I/O tests (PowerShell)
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-round-trip.sh` - FUSE write → encrypt → IPFS → API visibility
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-conflict-detection.sh` - Cross-device conflict resolution
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-recycle-bin.sh` - Soft-delete and bin recovery
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-cross-client-sync.sh` - SDK edit detection via FUSE mount
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/wait-for-mount.sh` - Mount readiness polling
- `/tests/desktop-e2e/fixtures/` - Crypto test vectors (JSON)

**Rust FUSE crate location:** `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/crates/fuse/src/`

**CI/CD workflow:** `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml`

---

### 2. **Testing Framework & Infrastructure**

**Architecture:** Shell-based integration tests that exercise the entire stack:
- Real FUSE mount (FUSE-T on macOS, kernel FUSE on Linux, WinFSP on Windows)
- Real encrypted vault operations
- Mock IPNS routing server (required for IPNS publish/resolve in CI)
- Real PostgreSQL, Redis, and Kubo (IPFS) services
- Test-login API endpoint for credential generation

**No unit test harness** — all tests are shell scripts exercising the mounted FUSE filesystem and API endpoints directly.

**The FUSE implementation itself is pure Rust** (in `crates/fuse/`), but e2e tests drive it through the filesystem interface, not via direct Rust function calls.

---

### 3. **Test Setup & Teardown Pattern**

**Mount Initialization** (`/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/mod.rs:49-200`):
1. Create mount point at `~/CipherBox` (cleaned on each run)
2. Create temp directory for file uploads (`/tmp/cipherbox`)
3. Initialize `InodeTable` with root inode marked as `InodeKind::Root`
4. Set root's IPNS name and private key from vault
5. Create 4 inter-process `mpsc` channels for background results:
   - `refresh_tx/rx` — metadata refresh completions
   - `content_tx/rx` — prefetched content completions
   - `filepointer_tx/rx` — resolved file pointer metadata
   - `upload_tx/rx` — file upload completions
6. Pre-populate root folder by resolving root IPNS → fetch encrypted bytes → decrypt metadata → populate inodes
7. Initialize `PublishCoordinator` with resolved sequence numbers (prevents 409 conflicts)
8. Mount FUSE filesystem via `fuser` crate and spawn FUSE event loop on background thread

**Vault Initialization** (test-login flow):
- Shell script uses `/auth/test-login` API endpoint with `TEST_SECRET` env var
- Server creates deterministic test account with seeded keypair
- Returns `accessToken`, `privateKeyHex`, `publicKeyHex`
- Desktop app uses returned keys to decrypt vault root folder key

**FUSE Key Generation:**
- Root folder key: 32 bytes AES-256 key (from vault settings)
- Root IPNS keypair: Ed25519 pair derived from vault seed
- File IPNS keypairs: Generated on file creation via `deriveVaultIpnsKeypair()`
- All encryption uses AES-256-GCM or AES-256-CTR depending on file type

**Key Files Referenced:**
- `/crates/fuse/src/lib.rs:536` — `CipherBoxFS` struct definition
- `/crates/fuse/src/lib.rs:573-650` — Initialization, metadata building, folder population
- `/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/mod.rs:50-200` — Desktop-specific mount orchestration

**Teardown:**
- Unmount filesystem:
  - macOS: `diskutil unmount force ~/CipherBox`
  - Linux: `fusermount3 -u ~/CipherBox`
  - Windows: `winfsp-service` API call
- Kill desktop process
- Database reset (migrations re-run)
- Clean temp directories

---

### 4. **Example E2E Tests with Filesystem Operations**

#### **Test: Create File** (`test-fuse-operations.sh:42-51`)
```bash
echo "Hello from CI" > "$MP/e2e-test.txt"
sleep 3
CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/e2e-test.txt")
if [ "$CONTENT" = "Hello from CI" ]; then
  pass "Create and read text file"
else
  fail "Create and read text file (got: '$CONTENT')"
fi
```
**Flow:** Write → async encrypt+upload on `release()` → 3s debounce → IPNS publish → readdir drains completions → subsequent read finds updated metadata

#### **Test: Rename File** (`test-fuse-operations.sh:97-110`)
```bash
mv "$MP/e2e-test.txt" "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt"
sleep 2
if [ ! -f "$MP/e2e-test.txt" ] && [ -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" ]; then
  RENAMED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt")
  if [ "$RENAMED" = "Modified content" ]; then
    pass "Rename file"
  else
    fail "Rename file (content changed: '$RENAMED')"
  fi
else
  fail "Rename file (old exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-test.txt" && echo yes || echo no), new exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" && echo yes || echo no))"
fi
```
**Flow (via FUSE callback `handle_rename()`)**:
1. Lookup source inode by name in parent directory
2. If destination exists and is different: remove or validate compatibility
3. Remove source from old parent's name index (NFC-normalized)
4. Update source inode's name and parent_ino
5. Update name lookup index for new location
6. Remove from old parent's children list, add to new parent's children list
7. Update both parents' mtime/ctime and trigger `update_folder_metadata()`
8. Queue both folders for debounced IPNS publish

**Code location:** `/crates/fuse/src/write_ops.rs:794-978`

#### **Test: Mkdir** (`test-fuse-operations.sh:53-60`)
```bash
mkdir -p "$MP/e2e-folder"
if [ -d "$MP/e2e-folder" ]; then
  pass "Create directory"
else
  fail "Create directory"
fi
```
**FUSE handler:** `handle_mkdir()` in `write_ops.rs`
- Creates new folder inode with unique IPNS keypair
- Adds to parent's children list
- Generates encrypted folder key and wrapped IPNS private key
- Updates parent folder metadata
- Queues parent for IPNS publish

#### **Test: Delete File** (`test-fuse-operations.sh:112-120`)
```bash
rm "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt"
sleep 2
if [ ! -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" ]; then
  pass "Delete file"
else
  fail "Delete file (still exists)"
fi
```
**FUSE handler:** `handle_unlink()` in `write_ops.rs`
- Removes file inode from parent's children
- Unpin file CID from IPFS (async)
- Update parent folder metadata
- Queue parent for debounced IPNS publish

#### **Test: Binary Round-Trip** (`test-fuse-operations.sh:84-95`)
```bash
dd if=/dev/urandom of=/tmp/e2e-binary.bin bs=1024 count=256 2>/dev/null
cp /tmp/e2e-binary.bin "$MP/e2e-binary.bin"
sleep 5
CHECKSUM_ORIG=$($SHA256_CMD /tmp/e2e-binary.bin | cut -d' ' -f1)
CHECKSUM_MOUNT=$($SHA256_CMD "$MP/e2e-binary.bin" | cut -d' ' -f1)
if [ "$CHECKSUM_ORIG" = "$CHECKSUM_MOUNT" ]; then
  pass "Binary file round-trip (256KB)"
```
**Flow:**
1. Write 256KB random file → buffered in temp file on `write()` calls
2. On `release()` → encrypt with AES-256-GCM → upload to IPFS → record CID in file metadata
3. Publish file's per-file IPNS record with encrypted metadata
4. Update parent folder metadata (marks file with new CID)
5. Read retrieves from content cache (populated on first access) → decrypt → return bytes

---

### 5. **How Tests Are Run**

**CI Workflow** (`/.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml`):

```bash
# Build step
cargo build -p cipherbox-desktop --no-default-features --features fuse

# Run all tests (master orchestrator script)
bash tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/run-all.sh
```

**Exit codes:**
- Each test script returns the count of failed tests (0 = all passed)
- `run-all.sh` aggregates failures across all 6 test suites

**Environment variables:**
```bash
MOUNT_POINT="$HOME/CipherBox"              # FUSE mount location
API_URL="http://localhost:3000"            # Backend API
TEST_SECRET=[REDACTED]"      # test-login shared secret
RUST_LOG="info"                            # Desktop app log level
CIPHERBOX_API_URL="http://localhost:3000"  # (deprecated, set in .env)
```

**Platform-specific runs:**
- **macOS:** `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/run-all.sh`
- **Linux:** `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/run-all.sh`
- **Windows:** `/tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/run-all.ps1` (PowerShell)

**Manual run (local development):**
```bash
# Prerequisites: API running on localhost:3000, IPFS daemon, Redis, PostgreSQL
# Desktop app running with --dev-key flag

bash tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/run-all.sh
# or individual test
bash tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-fuse-operations.sh
```

---

### 6. **Test Helper Modules & Utilities**

**SDK Verification Script** (`/packages/sdk-core/scripts/verify-filepointer.mjs`):
- Authenticates via test-login
- Resolves file metadata from IPNS
- Downloads and decrypts file content
- Verifies byte-for-byte match with expected content
- **Used by:** `test-round-trip.sh` and `test-cross-client-sync.sh`

**SDK Edit Script** (`/packages/sdk-core/scripts/edit-filepointer.mjs`):
- Authenticates via test-login
- Loads folder metadata
- Encrypts new content with AES-256-GCM
- Uploads to IPFS
- Updates file's per-file IPNS record
- Republishes folder metadata with bumped modified_at
- **Used by:** `test-cross-client-sync.sh` to simulate other-client edits

**FUSE Helper Modules** (Rust):
- `/crates/fuse/src/inode.rs` — `InodeTable` with name lookup, children tracking, NFC normalization
- `/crates/fuse/src/cache.rs` — `MetadataCache` and `ContentCache` with TTL
- `/crates/fuse/src/file_handle.rs` — `OpenFileHandle` for temp-file-backed writes
- `/crates/fuse/src/helpers.rs` — Platform-special file filtering, UUID generation
- `/crates/fuse/src/constants.rs` — Block size, timeout, cache limits

**Key helper patterns:**
1. **`drain_upload_completions()`** (`/crates/fuse/src/lib.rs:653-672`) — Drain `mpsc` channel of completed uploads, update inode CIDs, populate content cache
2. **`drain_refresh_completions()`** (`/crates/fuse/src/lib.rs:696-785`) — Drain metadata refresh results, repopulate inodes from refreshed metadata
3. **`update_folder_metadata()`** (`/crates/fuse/src/lib.rs:646-651`) — Queue folder for debounced publish
4. **`PublishCoordinator`** (`/crates/fuse/src/lib.rs:192-210`) — Per-IPNS-name publish serialization, monotonic sequence number tracking
5. **`spawn_metadata_refresh()`** (`/crates/fuse/src/lib.rs:127-168`) — Background task to resolve IPNS, fetch encrypted bytes, decrypt metadata

---

### 7. **Test Suite Overview**

From `/tests/TESTING_STRATEGY.md`:

| Test Suite | Location | Files Tested | Sync Flow | Special Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **FUSE Operations** | `test-fuse-operations.sh/ps1` | create, mkdir, write, rename, delete, binary round-trip | Single-device | Tests filesystem I/O only, not metadata visibility |
| **Round-Trip** | `test-round-trip.sh` | write via FUSE → verify via SDK | FUSE write → API visibility | Verifies encryption pipeline and IPNS publish |
| **Conflict Detection** | `test-conflict-detection.sh` | write, bump-server-seq, write again | Multi-device 409 handling | Tests conflict resolution and retry logic |
| **Recycle Bin** | `test-recycle-bin.sh` | create, delete, verify soft-delete | Soft-delete with CID retention | Verifies file not unpinned immediately |
| **Cross-Client Sync** | `test-cross-client-sync.sh` | write via FUSE, edit via SDK, read via FUSE | Bidirectional sync | Tests IPNS polling and metadata refresh |

**Total tests:** ~25 assertions across all scripts
**Typical runtime:** 2-5 minutes per platform (6 platforms × 3 = 18+ minutes in CI)

---

### 8. **Key Implementation Details for Rename**

**Rename is implemented in:** `/crates/fuse/src/write_ops.rs:794-978`

**Critical aspects:**
1. **FUSE-T truncation handling** (line 821-859): Handles case where FUSE-T's SMB backend truncates long names. Falls back to suffix-matching if exact name not found.

2. **Destination handling** (line 869-919):
   - Self-replace (same ino) → no-op
   - File→File or Dir→Dir → allow with dest removal
   - File→Dir or Dir→File → error ENOTDIR/EISDIR
   - Destination folder must be empty (ENOTEMPTY check)
   - Unpin destination file's CID async if exists

3. **Name index management** (line 921-943):
   - Remove old entry from parent's NFC-normalized name lookup:
     ```rust
     let nfc_key: String = name_str.nfc().collect();
     fs.inodes.name_to_ino.remove(&(parent, nfc_key));
     ```
   - Update source inode's name and parent_ino
   - Add new entry with new name:
     ```rust
     let nfc_key: String = newname_str.nfc().collect();
     fs.inodes.name_to_ino.insert((newparent, nfc_key), source_ino);
     ```

4. **Parent tracking** (line 945-975):
   - If moving to different parent: remove from old children, add to new, update both parents' mtime/ctime, trigger metadata updates on both
   - If same parent (rename only): update parent's mtime/ctime, trigger one metadata update

5. **Metadata publish** (line 961-972): Queue both parents for debounced IPNS publish via `update_folder_metadata()`

**For adding rename tests:**
- Follow pattern of `test-fuse-operations.sh:97-110`
- Use `mv` command to trigger rename FUSE callback
- Add sleep for debounce (2-3s minimum)
- Verify both old path gone AND new path exists with original content
- Test cross-parent rename by moving files between directories
- Test rename with content change (write → rename → verify content unchanged)

---

### 9. **Running the Test Suite Locally**

**Prerequisites:**
```bash
# 1. Backend services
brew install postgresql@16 redis kubo    # macOS
# Linux: apt-get install postgresql-16 redis-server ipfs

# 2. FUSE driver
brew install --cask fuse-t               # macOS (or WinFSP on Windows)
# Linux: sudo apt-get install libfuse3-dev fuse3

# 3. Development tools
rustup default stable
pnpm install
```

**Run the test suite:**
```bash

# Terminal 2: Redis

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -uo pipefail
3	
4	# test-fuse-operations.sh -- Exercise FUSE mount file I/O operations.
5	#
6	# Usage: ./test-fuse-operations.sh [mount-point]
7	#   mount-point  Path to FUSE mount (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
8	#
9	# Tests: create, read, overwrite, mkdir, nested write, binary round-trip,
10	#        rename, delete file, delete directory.
11	#
12	# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
13	
14	MP="${1:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
15	PASS=0
16	FAIL=0
17	
18	# Detect SHA-256 command (macOS vs Linux)
19	if command -v shasum &>/dev/null; then
20	  SHA256_CMD="shasum -a 256"
21	elif command -v sha256sum &>/dev/null; then
22	  SHA256_CMD="sha256sum"
23	else
24	  echo "FAIL: No SHA-256 command found (shasum or sha256sum)"
25	  exit 1
26	fi
27	
28	pass() {
29	  PASS=$((PASS + 1))
30	  echo "PASS: $1"
31	}
32	
33	fail() {
34	  FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
35	  echo "FAIL: $1"
36	}
37	
38	echo "=== FUSE File I/O Tests ==="
39	echo "Mount point: $MP"
40	echo ""
41	
42	# ---- Test 1: Create and read text file ----
43	echo "--- Test 1: Create and read text file ---"
44	echo "Hello from CI" > "$MP/e2e-test.txt"
45	sleep 3
46	CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/e2e-test.txt")
47	if [ "$CONTENT" = "Hello from CI" ]; then
48	  pass "Create and read text file"
49	else
50	  fail "Create and read text file (got: '$CONTENT')"
51	fi
52	
53	# ---- Test 2: Create directory ----
54	echo "--- Test 2: Create directory ---"
55	mkdir -p "$MP/e2e-folder"
56	if [ -d "$MP/e2e-folder" ]; then
57	  pass "Create directory"
58	else
59	  fail "Create directory"
60	fi
61	
62	# ---- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ----
63	echo "--- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ---"
64	echo "Nested content" > "$MP/e2e-folder/nested.txt"
65	sleep 3
66	NESTED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-folder/nested.txt")
67	if [ "$NESTED" = "Nested content" ]; then
68	  pass "Write file in subdirectory"
69	else
70	  fail "Write file in subdirectory (got: '$NESTED')"
71	fi
72	
73	# ---- Test 4: Overwrite file (modify) ----
74	echo "--- Test 4: Overwrite file ---"
75	echo "Modified content" > "$MP/e2e-test.txt"
76	sleep 3
77	MODIFIED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-test.txt")
78	if [ "$MODIFIED" = "Modified content" ]; then
79	  pass "Overwrite file"
80	else
81	  fail "Overwrite file (got: '$MODIFIED')"
82	fi
83	
84	# ---- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ----
85	echo "--- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ---"
86	dd if=/dev/urandom of=/tmp/e2e-binary.bin bs=1024 count=256 2>/dev/null
87	cp /tmp/e2e-binary.bin "$MP/e2e-binary.bin"
88	sleep 5
89	CHECKSUM_ORIG=$($SHA256_CMD /tmp/e2e-binary.bin | cut -d' ' -f1)
90	CHECKSUM_MOUNT=$($SHA256_CMD "$MP/e2e-binary.bin" | cut -d' ' -f1)
91	if [ "$CHECKSUM_ORIG" = "$CHECKSUM_MOUNT" ]; then
92	  pass "Binary file round-trip (256KB)"
93	else
94	  fail "Binary file round-trip (orig: $CHECKSUM_ORIG, mount: $CHECKSUM_MOUNT)"
95	fi
96	
97	# ---- Test 6: Rename file ----
98	echo "--- Test 6: Rename file ---"
99	mv "$MP/e2e-test.txt" "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt"
100	sleep 2
101	if [ ! -f "$MP/e2e-test.txt" ] && [ -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" ]; then
102	  RENAMED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt")
103	  if [ "$RENAMED" = "Modified content" ]; then
104	    pass "Rename file"
105	  else
106	    fail "Rename file (content changed: '$RENAMED')"
107	  fi
108	else
109	  fail "Rename file (old exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-test.txt" && echo yes || echo no), new exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" && echo yes || echo no))"
110	fi
111	
112	# ---- Test 7: Delete file ----
113	echo "--- Test 7: Delete file ---"
114	rm "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt"
115	sleep 2
116	if [ ! -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" ]; then
117	  pass "Delete file"
118	else
119	  fail "Delete file (still exists)"
120	fi
121	
122	# ---- Test 8: Delete directory ----
123	echo "--- Test 8: Delete directory ---"
124	rm -rf "$MP/e2e-folder"
125	sleep 2
126	if [ ! -d "$MP/e2e-folder" ]; then
127	  pass "Delete directory"
128	else
129	  fail "Delete directory (still exists)"
130	fi
131	
132	# ---- Test 9: Cleanup ----
133	echo "--- Test 9: Cleanup ---"
134	rm -f "$MP/e2e-binary.bin" 2>/dev/null || true
135	rm -f /tmp/e2e-binary.bin 2>/dev/null || true
136	# Filter .DS_Store from any remaining file checks
137	REMAINING=$(ls "$MP" 2>/dev/null | grep -v '\.DS_Store' | grep -v '^$' || true)
138	if [ -z "$REMAINING" ]; then
139	  pass "Cleanup (mount point clean)"
140	else
141	  # Not a hard failure -- other files may be present from the desktop app
142	  pass "Cleanup (remaining files: $REMAINING)"
143	fi
144	
145	# ---- Summary ----
146	echo ""
147	echo "=== FUSE File I/O Results ==="
148	echo "  Passed: $PASS"
149	echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
150	echo "=============================="
151	
152	exit "$FAIL"
153

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -uo pipefail
3	
4	# test-cross-client-sync.sh -- Verify FUSE mount picks up file edits made via SDK.
5	#
6	# Usage: ./test-cross-client-sync.sh [mount-point] [api-url]
7	#   mount-point  Path to FUSE mount (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
8	#   api-url      Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
9	#
10	# Environment:
11	#   TEST_SECRET  Shared secret for test-login (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
12	#
13	# This test exercises the cross-client sync path:
14	# 1. Write a file via the FUSE mount (desktop client)
15	# 2. Edit the file via the SDK (simulating a web UI edit)
16	# 3. Wait for the FUSE mount to detect the change (IPNS polling)
17	# 4. Read the file from the FUSE mount and verify updated content
18	#
19	# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
20	
21	MP="${1:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
22	API_URL="${2:-http://localhost:3000}"
23	SECRET="${TEST_SECRET=[REDACTED]}"
24	TEST_EMAIL="dev-key@cipherbox.local"
25	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
26	REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../../.." && pwd)"
27	TEST_FILE="sync-test-${RANDOM}.txt"
28	ORIGINAL_CONTENT="Original content from FUSE mount"
29	EDITED_CONTENT="Edited content from SDK client"
30	
31	PASS=0
32	FAIL=0
33	
34	pass() {
35	  PASS=$((PASS + 1))
36	  echo "PASS: $1"
37	}
38	
39	fail() {
40	  FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
41	  echo "FAIL: $1"
42	}
43	
44	ensure_runtime() {
45	  if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/dist/index.mjs" ] && \
46	     [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/api-client/dist/index.mjs" ] && \
47	     [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/core/dist/index.mjs" ] && \
48	     [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/crypto/dist/index.mjs" ]; then
49	    return 0
50	  fi
51	
52	  echo "Building SDK dependencies..."
53	  pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/crypto build && \
54	    pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/core build && \
55	    pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/api-client build && \
56	    pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/sdk-core build
57	}
58	
59	run_verify() {
60	  TEST_SECRET="$SECRET" node "$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/scripts/verify-filepointer.mjs" \
61	    --api-url "$API_URL" \
62	    --email "$TEST_EMAIL" \
63	    --file-name "$TEST_FILE" \
64	    --expected-content "$1"
65	}
66	
67	run_edit() {
68	  TEST_SECRET="$SECRET" node "$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/scripts/edit-filepointer.mjs" \
69	    --api-url "$API_URL" \
70	    --email "$TEST_EMAIL" \
71	    --file-name "$TEST_FILE" \
72	    --new-content "$1"
73	}
74	
75	echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Tests ==="
76	echo "Mount point: $MP"
77	echo "API URL:     $API_URL"
78	echo "Test email:  $TEST_EMAIL"
79	echo "Test file:   $TEST_FILE"
80	echo ""
81	
82	ensure_runtime || {
83	  echo "FATAL: Failed to build SDK dependencies."
84	  exit 1
85	}
86	
87	# ---- Test 1: Write file via FUSE mount ----
88	echo "--- Test 1: Write file via FUSE mount ---"
89	printf '%s' "$ORIGINAL_CONTENT" > "$MP/$TEST_FILE"
90	sleep 2
91	# Trigger FUSE readdir to drain upload completions
92	ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
93	
94	FUSE_CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo "")
95	if [ "$FUSE_CONTENT" = "$ORIGINAL_CONTENT" ]; then
96	  pass "Write file via FUSE mount"
97	else
98	  fail "Write file via FUSE mount (got: '$FUSE_CONTENT')"
99	fi
100	
101	# ---- Test 2: Wait for file to be visible via SDK ----
102	echo "--- Test 2: Wait for file to be visible via SDK ---"
103	SDK_VERIFY_OK=0
104	for attempt in $(seq 1 24); do
105	  # Trigger FUSE readdir each iteration to drain upload completions
106	  stat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
107	  ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
108	  sleep 5
109	  if VERIFY_OUTPUT=$(run_verify "$ORIGINAL_CONTENT" 2>&1); then
110	    SDK_VERIFY_OK=1
111	    break
112	  fi
113	  echo "  SDK verify attempt $attempt: $VERIFY_OUTPUT"
114	done
115	
116	if [ "$SDK_VERIFY_OK" -eq 1 ]; then
117	  pass "File visible and decryptable via SDK"
118	  echo "  $VERIFY_OUTPUT"
119	else
120	  fail "File not visible via SDK after 120s"
121	  echo "FATAL: Cannot proceed without SDK-visible file."
122	  echo ""
123	  echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Results ==="
124	  echo "  Passed: $PASS"
125	  echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
126	  echo "=================================="
127	  # Cleanup
128	  rm -f "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
129	  exit "$FAIL"
130	fi
131	
132	# ---- Test 3: Edit file via SDK ----
133	echo "--- Test 3: Edit file via SDK ---"
134	if EDIT_OUTPUT=$(run_edit "$EDITED_CONTENT" 2>&1); then
135	  pass "Edit file via SDK"
136	  echo "  $EDIT_OUTPUT"
137	else
138	  fail "Edit file via SDK ($EDIT_OUTPUT)"
139	  echo "FATAL: Cannot test sync without successful edit."
140	  echo ""
141	  echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Results ==="
142	  echo "  Passed: $PASS"
143	  echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
144	  echo "=================================="
145	  # Cleanup
146	  rm -f "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
147	  exit "$FAIL"
148	fi
149	
150	# ---- Test 4: Verify edit persisted via SDK ----
151	echo "--- Test 4: Verify edit persisted via SDK ---"
152	sleep 2
153	if VERIFY_EDIT_OUTPUT=$(run_verify "$EDITED_CONTENT" 2>&1); then
154	  pass "Edited content verified via SDK"
155	  echo "  $VERIFY_EDIT_OUTPUT"
156	else
157	  fail "Edited content not verified via SDK ($VERIFY_EDIT_OUTPUT)"
158	fi
159	
160	# ---- Test 5: Wait for FUSE mount to pick up edited content ----
161	echo "--- Test 5: Wait for FUSE mount to detect edit ---"
162	# The FUSE mount polls folder metadata every 30s. After detecting a newer
163	# modified_at on the FilePointer, it re-resolves the file's IPNS record
164	# to pick up the new CID. Allow up to 120s for two full polling cycles.
165	SYNC_OK=0
166	for attempt in $(seq 1 24); do
167	  sleep 5
168	  # Trigger readdir to reliably drain refresh completions via
169	  # drain_refresh_completions() -> populate_folder(), which detects
170	  # modified_at changes and marks files for re-resolution. We use `ls`
171	  # here because it is a dependable refresh trigger during polling.
172	  ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
173	  stat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
174	  FUSE_CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo "")
175	  if [ "$FUSE_CONTENT" = "$EDITED_CONTENT" ]; then
176	    SYNC_OK=1
177	    echo "  Sync detected on attempt $attempt ($(( attempt * 5 ))s)"
178	    break
179	  fi
180	  echo "  Sync poll attempt $attempt: content still original"
181	done
182	
183	if [ "$SYNC_OK" -eq 1 ]; then
184	  pass "FUSE mount picked up edited content"
185	else
186	  fail "FUSE mount still shows original content after 120s (got: '$FUSE_CONTENT')"
187	fi
188	
189	# ---- Cleanup ----
190	echo "--- Cleanup ---"
191	rm -f "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
192	sleep 2
193	
194	# ---- Summary ----
195	echo ""
196	echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Results ==="
197	echo "  Passed: $PASS"
198	echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
199	echo "=================================="
200	
201	exit "$FAIL"
202	
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	
4	# run-all.sh -- Orchestrate the full desktop E2E test suite.
5	#
6	# Runs wait-for-mount, FUSE file I/O tests, and API round-trip tests.
7	# Reports an aggregate pass/fail summary and exits with the total failure count.
8	#
9	# Environment:
10	#   MOUNT_POINT   Path to FUSE mount (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
11	#   API_URL       Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
12	#   TEST_SECRET   test-login shared secret (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
13	
14	MOUNT_POINT="${MOUNT_POINT:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
15	API_URL="${API_URL:-http://localhost:3000}"
16	TEST_SECRET="${TEST_SECRET=[REDACTED]}"
17	
18	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
19	
20	TOTAL_PASS=0
21	TOTAL_FAIL=0
22	
23	echo "============================================"
24	echo "  CipherBox Desktop E2E Test Suite"
25	echo "============================================"
26	echo ""
27	echo "Mount point: $MOUNT_POINT"
28	echo "API URL:     $API_URL"
29	echo ""
30	
31	# ---- Step 1: Wait for mount ----
32	echo "--- Step 1: Wait for mount ---"
33	if bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/wait-for-mount.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT"; then
34	  TOTAL_PASS=$((TOTAL_PASS + 1))
35	else
36	  echo "FATAL: Mount not available. Cannot continue."
37	  exit 1
38	fi
39	echo ""
40	
41	# ---- Step 2: FUSE file operations ----
42	echo "--- Step 2: FUSE file operations ---"
43	set +e
44	bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/test-fuse-operations.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT"
45	FUSE_FAILURES=$?
46	set -e
47	
48	if [ "$FUSE_FAILURES" -eq 0 ]; then
49	  echo "FUSE operations: ALL PASSED"
50	else
51	  echo "FUSE operations: $FUSE_FAILURES FAILURE(S)"
52	  TOTAL_FAIL=$((TOTAL_FAIL + FUSE_FAILURES))
53	fi
54	echo ""
55	
56	# ---- Step 3: API round-trip ----
57	echo "--- Step 3: API round-trip ---"
58	set +e
59	TEST_SECRET="$TEST_SECRET" bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/test-round-trip.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT" "$API_URL"
60	RT_FAILURES=$?
61	set -e
62	
63	if [ "$RT_FAILURES" -eq 0 ]; then
64	  echo "API round-trip: ALL PASSED"
65	else
66	  echo "API round-trip: $RT_FAILURES FAILURE(S)"
67	  TOTAL_FAIL=$((TOTAL_FAIL + RT_FAILURES))
68	fi
69	echo ""
70	
71	# ---- Step 4: Conflict detection ----
72	echo "--- Step 4: Conflict detection ---"
73	set +e
74	TEST_SECRET="$TEST_SECRET" bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/test-conflict-detection.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT" "$API_URL"
75	CONFLICT_FAILURES=$?
76	set -e
77	
78	if [ "$CONFLICT_FAILURES" -eq 0 ]; then
79	  echo "Conflict detection: ALL PASSED"
80	else
81	  echo "Conflict detection: $CONFLICT_FAILURES FAILURE(S)"
82	  TOTAL_FAIL=$((TOTAL_FAIL + CONFLICT_FAILURES))
83	fi
84	echo ""
85	
86	# ---- Step 5: Recycle bin ----
87	echo "--- Step 5: Recycle bin ---"
88	set +e
89	TEST_SECRET="$TEST_SECRET" bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/test-recycle-bin.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT" "$API_URL"
90	BIN_FAILURES=$?
91	set -e
92	
93	if [ "$BIN_FAILURES" -eq 0 ]; then
94	  echo "Recycle bin: ALL PASSED"
95	else
96	  echo "Recycle bin: $BIN_FAILURES FAILURE(S)"
97	  TOTAL_FAIL=$((TOTAL_FAIL + BIN_FAILURES))
98	fi
99	echo ""
100	
101	# ---- Step 6: Cross-client sync ----
102	echo "--- Step 6: Cross-client sync ---"
103	set +e
104	TEST_SECRET="$TEST_SECRET" bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/test-cross-client-sync.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT" "$API_URL"
105	SYNC_FAILURES=$?
106	set -e
107	
108	if [ "$SYNC_FAILURES" -eq 0 ]; then
109	  echo "Cross-client sync: ALL PASSED"
110	else
111	  echo "Cross-client sync: $SYNC_FAILURES FAILURE(S)"
112	  TOTAL_FAIL=$((TOTAL_FAIL + SYNC_FAILURES))
113	fi
114	echo ""
115	
116	# ---- Summary ----
117	echo "============================================"
118	echo "  Summary"
119	echo "============================================"
120	echo "  Total failures: $TOTAL_FAIL"
121	echo "============================================"
122	
123	exit "$TOTAL_FAIL"
124

1	# test-fuse-operations.ps1 -- Exercise FUSE mount file I/O operations (Windows).
2	#
3	# Usage: .\test-fuse-operations.ps1 [-MountPoint <path>]
4	#   -MountPoint  Path to FUSE mount (default: $env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox)
5	#
6	# Tests: create, read, overwrite, mkdir, nested write, binary round-trip,
7	#        rename, delete file, delete directory.
8	#
9	# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
10	
11	param(
12	    [string]$MountPoint = "$env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox"
13	)
14	
15	$ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"
16	
17	$Pass = 0
18	$Fail = 0
19	
20	function Test-Pass {
21	    param([string]$Name)
22	    $script:Pass++
23	    Write-Host "PASS: $Name"
24	}
25	
26	function Test-Fail {
27	    param([string]$Name)
28	    $script:Fail++
29	    Write-Host "FAIL: $Name"
30	}
31	
32	Write-Host "=== FUSE File I/O Tests ==="
33	Write-Host "Mount point: $MountPoint"
34	Write-Host ""
35	
36	# ---- Test 1: Create and read text file ----
37	Write-Host "--- Test 1: Create and read text file ---"
38	try {
39	    Set-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Value "Hello from CI" -NoNewline -ErrorAction Stop
40	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
41	    $Content = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Raw -ErrorAction Stop
42	    if ($null -ne $Content -and $Content.Trim() -eq "Hello from CI") {
43	        Test-Pass "Create and read text file"
44	    } else {
45	        Test-Fail "Create and read text file (got: '$Content')"
46	    }
47	} catch {
48	    Test-Fail "Create and read text file (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
49	}
50	
51	# ---- Test 2: Create directory ----
52	Write-Host "--- Test 2: Create directory ---"
53	try {
54	    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder" -Force -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
55	    if (Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder" -PathType Container) {
56	        Test-Pass "Create directory"
57	    } else {
58	        Test-Fail "Create directory"
59	    }
60	} catch {
61	    Test-Fail "Create directory (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
62	}
63	
64	# ---- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ----
65	Write-Host "--- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ---"
66	try {
67	    Set-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder\nested.txt" -Value "Nested content" -NoNewline -ErrorAction Stop
68	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
69	    $Nested = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder\nested.txt" -Raw -ErrorAction Stop
70	    if ($null -ne $Nested -and $Nested.Trim() -eq "Nested content") {
71	        Test-Pass "Write file in subdirectory"
72	    } else {
73	        Test-Fail "Write file in subdirectory (got: '$Nested')"
74	    }
75	} catch {
76	    Test-Fail "Write file in subdirectory (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
77	}
78	
79	# ---- Test 4: Overwrite file (modify) ----
80	Write-Host "--- Test 4: Overwrite file ---"
81	try {
82	    Set-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Value "Modified content" -NoNewline -ErrorAction Stop
83	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
84	    $Modified = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Raw -ErrorAction Stop
85	    if ($null -ne $Modified -and $Modified.Trim() -eq "Modified content") {
86	        Test-Pass "Overwrite file"
87	    } else {
88	        Test-Fail "Overwrite file (got: '$Modified')"
89	    }
90	} catch {
91	    Test-Fail "Overwrite file (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
92	}
93	
94	# ---- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ----
95	Write-Host "--- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ---"
96	try {
97	    $TempBinary = "$env:TEMP\e2e-binary.bin"
98	    $MountBinary = "$MountPoint\e2e-binary.bin"
99	    $Bytes = New-Object byte[] (256 * 1024)
100	    $Rng = [System.Security.Cryptography.RNGCryptoServiceProvider]::new()
101	    $Rng.GetBytes($Bytes)
102	    $Rng.Dispose()
103	    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($TempBinary, $Bytes)
104	    Copy-Item -Path $TempBinary -Destination $MountBinary -ErrorAction Stop
105	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
106	    $HashOrig = (Get-FileHash -Path $TempBinary -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
107	    $HashMount = (Get-FileHash -Path $MountBinary -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
108	    if ($HashOrig -eq $HashMount) {
109	        Test-Pass "Binary file round-trip (256KB)"
110	    } else {
111	        Test-Fail "Binary file round-trip (orig: $HashOrig, mount: $HashMount)"
112	    }
113	} catch {
114	    Test-Fail "Binary file round-trip (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
115	}
116	
117	# ---- Test 6: Rename file ----
118	Write-Host "--- Test 6: Rename file ---"
119	try {
120	    Move-Item -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Destination "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt" -Force -ErrorAction Stop
121	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
122	    $OldExists = Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt"
123	    $NewExists = Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt"
124	    if (-not $OldExists -and $NewExists) {
125	        $Renamed = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt" -Raw
126	        if ($Renamed.Trim() -eq "Modified content") {
127	            Test-Pass "Rename file"
128	        } else {
129	            Test-Fail "Rename file (content changed: '$Renamed')"
130	        }
131	    } else {
132	        Test-Fail "Rename file (old exists: $OldExists, new exists: $NewExists)"
133	    }
134	} catch {
135	    Test-Fail "Rename file (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
136	}
137	
138	# ---- Test 7: Delete file ----
139	Write-Host "--- Test 7: Delete file ---"
140	try {
141	    Remove-Item -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt" -Force -ErrorAction Stop
142	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
143	    if (-not (Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt")) {
144	        Test-Pass "Delete file"
145	    } else {
146	        Test-Fail "Delete file (still exists)"
147	    }
148	} catch {
149	    Test-Fail "Delete file (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
150	}
151	
152	# ---- Test 8: Delete directory ----
153	Write-Host "--- Test 8: Delete directory ---"
154	try {
155	    # Delete known files first, then the empty directory (most reliable on FUSE)
156	    Remove-Item -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder\nested.txt" -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
157	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
158	
159	    # Verify directory is now empty
160	    $Children = @(Get-ChildItem -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder" -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
161	    Write-Host "  Directory children after nested.txt delete: $($Children.Count) items"
162	    foreach ($c in $Children) { Write-Host "    - $($c.Name)" }
163	
164	    # Use [System.IO.Directory]::Delete which calls RemoveDirectoryW directly,
165	    # avoiding PowerShell provider overhead that can fail on WinFsp mounts.
166	    [System.IO.Directory]::Delete("$MountPoint\e2e-folder")
167	    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
168	    if (-not (Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder")) {
169	        Test-Pass "Delete directory"
170	    } else {
171	        Test-Fail "Delete directory (still exists)"
172	    }
173	} catch {
174	    Write-Host "  Directory::Delete failed: $($_.Exception.Message)"
175	    Write-Host "  Trying cmd /c rd fallback..."
176	    try {
177	        cmd /c rd "$MountPoint\e2e-folder" 2>&1
178	        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
179	        if (-not (Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder")) {
180	            Test-Pass "Delete directory (via rd fallback)"
181	        } else {
182	            Test-Fail "Delete directory (still exists after rd)"
183	        }
184	    } catch {
185	        Test-Fail "Delete directory (exception: $($_.Exception.Message))"
186	    }
187	}
188	
189	# ---- Test 9: Cleanup ----
190	Write-Host "--- Test 9: Cleanup ---"
191	Remove-Item -Path $MountBinary -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
192	Remove-Item -Path $TempBinary -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
193	if ((-not (Test-Path $MountBinary)) -and (-not (Test-Path $TempBinary))) {
194	    Test-Pass "Cleanup"
195	} else {
196	    Test-Fail "Cleanup (artifacts still exist)"
197	}
198	
199	# ---- Summary ----
200	Write-Host ""
201	Write-Host "=== FUSE File I/O Results ==="
202	Write-Host "  Passed: $Pass"
203	Write-Host "  Failed: $Fail"
204	Write-Host "=============================="
205	
206	exit $Fail
207	
```

240	/**
241	 * Rename a child entry (folder or file) in folder metadata.
242	 *
243	 * Pure metadata operation: returns updated children array without publishing.
244	 */
245	export function renameInFolder(params: {
246	  children: FolderChild[];
247	  childId: string;
248	  newName: string;
249	}): { updatedChildren: FolderChild[]; renamedChild: FolderChild } {
250	  const children = [...params.children];
251	  const index = children.findIndex((c) => c.id === params.childId);
252	
253	  if (index === -1) throw new Error('Item not found');
254	
255	  const nameExists = children.some((c) => c.name === params.newName && c.id !== params.childId);
256	  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists');
257	
258	  const renamedChild = {
259	    ...children[index],
260	    name: params.newName,
261	    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
262	  };
263	  children[index] = renamedChild;
264	
265	  return { updatedChildren: children, renamedChild };
266	}
267	
268	/**
269	 * Remove a child entry (folder or file) from folder metadata.

1	/**
2	 * edit-filepointer.mjs -- Edit a file's content via the SDK.
3	 *
4	 * Authenticates via test-login, finds the target file in the vault,
5	 * encrypts new content, uploads to IPFS, updates the file's IPNS
6	 * metadata record, and republishes folder metadata with an updated
7	 * modified_at timestamp so other clients (e.g. FUSE mount) detect
8	 * the change.
9	 *
10	 * Usage:
11	 *   TEST_SECRET=<secret> node edit-filepointer.mjs \
12	 *     --api-url http://localhost:3000 \
13	 *     --email dev-key@cipherbox.local \
14	 *     --file-name hello.txt \
15	 *     --new-content "Updated content from SDK"
16	 */
17	
18	import { createAxiosInstance } from '../../api-client/dist/index.mjs';
19	import {
20	  loadVaultKeyBlob,
21	  loadFolderMetadata,
22	  resolveFileMetadata,
23	  updateFileMetadata,
24	  replaceFileInFolder,
25	  updateFolderMetadataAndPublish,
26	} from '../dist/index.mjs';
27	import {
28	  encryptAesGcm,
29	  generateFileKey,
30	  generateIv,
31	  wrapKey,
32	  unwrapKey,
33	  bytesToHex,
34	  hexToBytes,
35	  deriveVaultIpnsKeypair,
36	  clearBytes,
37	} from '../../crypto/dist/index.mjs';
38	import { addToIpfs } from '../dist/index.mjs';
39	
40	function parseArgs(argv) {
41	  const values = new Map();
42	
43	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i += 1) {
44	    const token = argv[i];
45	    if (!token.startsWith('--')) {
46	      throw new Error(`Unexpected argument: ${token}`);
47	    }
48	
49	    const key = token.slice(2);
50	    const value = argv[i + 1];
51	    if (value === undefined || value.startsWith('--')) {
52	      throw new Error(`Missing value for --${key}`);
53	    }
54	
55	    values.set(key, value);
56	    i += 1;
57	  }
58	
59	  if (values.has('secret')) {
60	    throw new Error('Do not pass --secret on CLI. Set TEST_SECRET in environment.');
61	  }
62	
63	  const apiUrl = values.get('api-url');
64	  const secret = process.env.TEST_SECRET;
65	  const email = values.get('email');
66	  const fileName = values.get('file-name');
67	  const newContent = values.get('new-content');
68	
69	  if (!apiUrl || !email || !fileName || !secret || newContent === undefined) {
70	    throw new Error(
71	      'Usage: edit-filepointer.mjs --api-url <url> --email <email> --file-name <name> --new-content <text> (requires TEST_SECRET env var)'
72	    );
73	  }
74	
75	  return { apiUrl, secret, email, fileName, newContent };
76	}
77	
78	async function authenticate(apiUrl, email, secret) {
79	  const response = await fetch(`${apiUrl}/auth/test-login`, {
80	    method: 'POST',
81	    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
82	    body: JSON.stringify({ email, secret }),
83	  });
84	
85	  if (!response.ok) {
86	    const body = await response.text();
87	    throw new Error(`test-login failed (${response.status}): ${body}`);
88	  }
89	
90	  const payload = await response.json();
91	
92	  if (!payload.accessToken || !payload.privateKeyHex || !payload.publicKeyHex) {
93	    throw new Error('test-login response missing accessToken, privateKeyHex, or publicKeyHex');
94	  }
95	
96	  return payload;
97	}
98	
99	async function main() {
100	  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
101	  const auth = await authenticate(args.apiUrl, args.email, args.secret);
102	  const accessToken = auth.accessToken;
103	  const userPrivateKey = Uint8Array.from(Buffer.from(auth.privateKeyHex, 'hex'));
104	  const userPublicKey = Uint8Array.from(Buffer.from(auth.publicKeyHex, 'hex'));
105	
106	  const axiosInstance = createAxiosInstance({
107	    baseUrl: args.apiUrl,
108	    getAccessToken: async () => accessToken,
109	  });
110	
111	  const ctx = {
112	    apiUrl: args.apiUrl,
113	    getAccessToken: async () => accessToken,
114	    axiosInstance,
115	  };
116	
117	  // 1. Load vault key blob to get rootFolderKey
118	  const vaultKeyBlob = await loadVaultKeyBlob({ userPrivateKey, ctx });
119	  if (!vaultKeyBlob) {
120	    throw new Error('Vault key blob not found');
121	  }
122	
123	  // 2. Get vault info for rootIpnsName
124	  const vaultResponse = await axiosInstance.get('/vault');
125	  const rootIpnsName = vaultResponse.data.rootIpnsName;
126	  if (!rootIpnsName) {
127	    throw new Error('Vault response missing rootIpnsName');
128	  }
129	
130	  // 3. Load root folder metadata
131	  const folder = await loadFolderMetadata({
132	    ipnsName: rootIpnsName,
133	    folderKey: vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
134	    ctx,
135	  });
136	  if (!folder) {
137	    throw new Error(`Root folder metadata not found for ${rootIpnsName}`);
138	  }
139	
140	  // 4. Find the target file's FilePointer
141	  const filePointer = folder.metadata.children.find(
142	    (child) => child.type === 'file' && child.name === args.fileName
143	  );
144	  if (!filePointer) {
145	    throw new Error(`FilePointer not found for ${args.fileName}`);
146	  }
147	  if (!filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName) {
148	    throw new Error(`FilePointer for ${args.fileName} is missing fileMetaIpnsName`);
149	  }
150	
151	  // 5. Resolve current file metadata
152	  const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
153	    filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
154	    vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
155	    ctx
156	  );
157	
158	  // 6. Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer
159	  if (!filePointer.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted) {
160	    throw new Error('FilePointer missing ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted (legacy files not supported)');
161	  }
162	  const fileIpnsPrivateKey = await unwrapKey(
163	    hexToBytes(filePointer.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted),
164	    userPrivateKey
165	  );
166	
167	  // 7. Encrypt new content
168	  const plaintext = new TextEncoder().encode(args.newContent);
169	  const fileKey = generateFileKey();
170	  const iv = generateIv();
171	  let newCid;
172	  let wrappedKeyHex;
173	  let ivHex;
174	
175	  try {
176	    const ciphertext = await encryptAesGcm(plaintext, fileKey, iv);
177	    const wrappedKey = await wrapKey(fileKey, userPublicKey);
178	    wrappedKeyHex = bytesToHex(wrappedKey);
179	    ivHex = bytesToHex(iv);
180	
181	    // 8. Upload encrypted content to IPFS
182	    const uploadResult = await addToIpfs(ctx, ciphertext);
183	    newCid = uploadResult.cid;
184	  } finally {
185	    clearBytes(fileKey);
186	  }
187	
188	  // 9. Update file metadata IPNS record
189	  let ipnsRecord;
190	  try {
191	    ({ ipnsRecord } = await updateFileMetadata({
192	      fileIpnsPrivateKey,
193	      fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
194	      folderKey: vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
195	      currentMetadata,
196	      updates: {
197	        cid: newCid,
198	        fileKeyEncrypted: wrappedKeyHex,
199	        fileIv: ivHex,
200	        size: plaintext.length,
201	        encryptionMode: 'GCM',
202	      },
203	      createVersion: true,
204	      ctx,
205	    }));
206	  } finally {
207	    fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
208	  }
209	
210	  // 10. Publish only the file IPNS record
211	  await replaceFileInFolder({
212	    children: folder.metadata.children,
213	    fileId: filePointer.id,
214	    fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
215	    ctx,
216	  });
217	
218	  // 11. Republish folder metadata with updated modified_at so other clients
219	  // (e.g. FUSE mount) detect the change via the FilePointer timestamp.
220	  const updatedChildren = folder.metadata.children.map((child) => {
221	    if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === filePointer.id) {
222	      return { ...child, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
223	    }
224	    return child;
225	  });
226	
227	  // Derive root folder IPNS keypair for republishing
228	  const rootIpnsKeypair = await deriveVaultIpnsKeypair(userPrivateKey);
229	
230	  const { newSequenceNumber } = await updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
231	    children: updatedChildren,
232	    folderKey: vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
233	    ipnsPrivateKey: rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
234	    ipnsName: rootIpnsName,
235	    sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
236	    ctx,
237	  });
238	
239	  // Zero sensitive key material
240	  rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
241	
242	  console.log(
243	    JSON.stringify({
244	      fileName: args.fileName,
245	      oldCid: currentMetadata.cid,
246	      newCid,
247	      newContentLength: plaintext.length,
248	      folderSequenceNumber: newSequenceNumber.toString(),
249	      fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
250	    })
251	  );
252	}
253	
254	main().catch((error) => {
255	  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
256	  console.error(message);
257	  process.exit(1);
258	});
259	
```

This exercises a bug fix where renaming a folder in the web UI and syncing to desktop created a duplicate folder instead of updating the name.\n\n## Task 1: Create `rename-folder.mjs`\n\nCreate `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/scripts/rename-folder.mjs` — an SDK script that renames a folder in the vault, simulating what the web UI does.\n\n**Model it closely on** `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/scripts/edit-filepointer.mjs` for the auth/vault loading boilerplate.\n\n**Usage:**\n```\nTEST_SECRET=<secret> node rename-folder.mjs \\\n  --api-url http://localhost:3000 \\\n  --email dev-key@cipherbox.local \\\n  --folder-name \"OldName\" \\\n  --new-name \"NewName\"\n```\n\n**Logic:**\n1. Authenticate via test-login (same as edit-filepointer.mjs)\n2. Load vault key blob to get rootFolderKey\n3. Get rootIpnsName from vault endpoint\n4. Load root folder metadata\n5. Republish folder metadata with updated children: `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({ children: updatedChildren, folderKey: vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey, ipnsPrivateKey: rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey, ipnsName: rootIpnsName, sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber, ctx })`\n9. Clear sensitive keys\n10. Create a folder via FUSE: `mkdir \"$MP/sync-rename-test\"`\n2. Create a file inside it: `echo \"rename test content\" > \"$MP/sync-rename-test/inner.txt\"`\n3. Sleep 3s, then trigger `ls` to flush uploads\n4. Wait for folder to be visible (simple stat check, retry loop)\n5. Rename folder via SDK: call `rename-folder.mjs --api-url ... --email ... --folder-name \"sync-rename-test\" --new-name \"sync-renamed-folder\"`\n6. Wait for FUSE mount to pick up the rename (poll loop, up to 120s):\n   - `ls \"$MP\"` to trigger metadata refresh\n   - Check if `sync-renamed-folder` exists AND `sync-rename-test` does NOT exist\n7. Verify nested content: `cat \"$MP/sync-renamed-folder/inner.txt\"` should be \"rename test content\"\n8. Cleanup: `rm -rf \"$MP/sync-renamed-folder\"`\n\nAdd a helper function `run_rename_folder` similar to the existing `run_edit` function:\n```bash\nrun_rename_folder() {\n  TEST_SECRET=\"$SECRET\" node \"$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/scripts/rename-folder.mjs\" \\\n    --api-url \"$API_URL\" \\\n    --email \"$TEST_EMAIL\" \\\n    --folder-name \"$1\" \\\n    --new-name \"$2\"\n}\n```\n\n### Test numbering\nThe existing tests are 1-5 + Cleanup. 1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -uo pipefail
3	
4	# test-fuse-operations.sh -- Exercise FUSE mount file I/O operations.
5	#
6	# Usage: ./test-fuse-operations.sh [mount-point]
7	#   mount-point  Path to FUSE mount (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
8	#
9	# Tests: create, read, overwrite, mkdir, nested write, binary round-trip,
10	#        rename file, rename directory, move file across directories,
11	#        delete file, delete directory.
12	#
13	# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
14	
15	MP="${1:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
16	PASS=0
17	FAIL=0
18	
19	# Detect SHA-256 command (macOS vs Linux)
20	if command -v shasum &>/dev/null; then
21	  SHA256_CMD="shasum -a 256"
22	elif command -v sha256sum &>/dev/null; then
23	  SHA256_CMD="sha256sum"
24	else
25	  echo "FAIL: No SHA-256 command found (shasum or sha256sum)"
26	  exit 1
27	fi
28	
29	pass() {
30	  PASS=$((PASS + 1))
31	  echo "PASS: $1"
32	}
33	
34	fail() {
35	  FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
36	  echo "FAIL: $1"
37	}
38	
39	echo "=== FUSE File I/O Tests ==="
40	echo "Mount point: $MP"
41	echo ""
42	
43	# ---- Test 1: Create and read text file ----
44	echo "--- Test 1: Create and read text file ---"
45	echo "Hello from CI" > "$MP/e2e-test.txt"
46	sleep 3
47	CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/e2e-test.txt")
48	if [ "$CONTENT" = "Hello from CI" ]; then
49	  pass "Create and read text file"
50	else
51	  fail "Create and read text file (got: '$CONTENT')"
52	fi
53	
54	# ---- Test 2: Create directory ----
55	echo "--- Test 2: Create directory ---"
56	mkdir -p "$MP/e2e-folder"
57	if [ -d "$MP/e2e-folder" ]; then
58	  pass "Create directory"
59	else
60	  fail "Create directory"
61	fi
62	
63	# ---- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ----
64	echo "--- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ---"
65	echo "Nested content" > "$MP/e2e-folder/nested.txt"
66	sleep 3
67	NESTED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-folder/nested.txt")
68	if [ "$NESTED" = "Nested content" ]; then
69	  pass "Write file in subdirectory"
70	else
71	  fail "Write file in subdirectory (got: '$NESTED')"
72	fi
73	
74	# ---- Test 4: Overwrite file (modify) ----
75	echo "--- Test 4: Overwrite file ---"
76	echo "Modified content" > "$MP/e2e-test.txt"
77	sleep 3
78	MODIFIED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-test.txt")
79	if [ "$MODIFIED" = "Modified content" ]; then
80	  pass "Overwrite file"
81	else
82	  fail "Overwrite file (got: '$MODIFIED')"
83	fi
84	
85	# ---- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ----
86	echo "--- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ---"
87	dd if=/dev/urandom of=/tmp/e2e-binary.bin bs=1024 count=256 2>/dev/null
88	cp /tmp/e2e-binary.bin "$MP/e2e-binary.bin"
89	sleep 5
90	CHECKSUM_ORIG=$($SHA256_CMD /tmp/e2e-binary.bin | cut -d' ' -f1)
91	CHECKSUM_MOUNT=$($SHA256_CMD "$MP/e2e-binary.bin" | cut -d' ' -f1)
92	if [ "$CHECKSUM_ORIG" = "$CHECKSUM_MOUNT" ]; then
93	  pass "Binary file round-trip (256KB)"
94	else
95	  fail "Binary file round-trip (orig: $CHECKSUM_ORIG, mount: $CHECKSUM_MOUNT)"
96	fi
97	
98	# ---- Test 6: Rename file ----
99	echo "--- Test 6: Rename file ---"
100	mv "$MP/e2e-test.txt" "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt"
101	sleep 2
102	if [ ! -f "$MP/e2e-test.txt" ] && [ -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" ]; then
103	  RENAMED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt")
104	  if [ "$RENAMED" = "Modified content" ]; then
105	    pass "Rename file"
106	  else
107	    fail "Rename file (content changed: '$RENAMED')"
108	  fi
109	else
110	  fail "Rename file (old exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-test.txt" && echo yes || echo no), new exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" && echo yes || echo no))"
111	fi
112	
113	# ---- Test 6b: Rename directory ----
114	echo "--- Test 6b: Rename directory ---"
115	mv "$MP/e2e-folder" "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed"
116	sleep 2
117	if [ ! -d "$MP/e2e-folder" ] && [ -d "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed" ]; then
118	  # Verify nested content is still accessible
119	  NESTED_AFTER=$(cat "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed/nested.txt")
120	  if [ "$NESTED_AFTER" = "Nested content" ]; then
121	    pass "Rename directory (with nested content preserved)"
122	  else
123	    fail "Rename directory (nested content changed: '$NESTED_AFTER')"
124	  fi
125	else
126	  fail "Rename directory (old exists: $(test -d "$MP/e2e-folder" && echo yes || echo no), new exists: $(test -d "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed" && echo yes || echo no))"
127	fi
128	
129	# ---- Test 6c: Move file across directories ----
130	echo "--- Test 6c: Move file across directories ---"
131	mkdir -p "$MP/e2e-dest-folder"
132	mv "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed/nested.txt" "$MP/e2e-dest-folder/nested.txt"
133	sleep 2
134	if [ ! -f "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed/nested.txt" ] && [ -f "$MP/e2e-dest-folder/nested.txt" ]; then
135	  MOVED=$(cat "$MP/e2e-dest-folder/nested.txt")
136	  if [ "$MOVED" = "Nested content" ]; then
137	    pass "Move file across directories"
138	  else
139	    fail "Move file across directories (content changed: '$MOVED')"
140	  fi
141	else
142	  fail "Move file across directories (source exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed/nested.txt" && echo yes || echo no), dest exists: $(test -f "$MP/e2e-dest-folder/nested.txt" && echo yes || echo no))"
143	fi
144	
145	# ---- Test 7: Delete file ----
146	echo "--- Test 7: Delete file ---"
147	rm "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt"
148	sleep 2
149	if [ ! -f "$MP/e2e-renamed.txt" ]; then
150	  pass "Delete file"
151	else
152	  fail "Delete file (still exists)"
153	fi
154	
155	# ---- Test 8: Delete directories ----
156	echo "--- Test 8: Delete directories ---"
157	rm -rf "$MP/e2e-dest-folder"
158	rm -rf "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed"
159	sleep 2
160	if [ ! -d "$MP/e2e-dest-folder" ] && [ ! -d "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed" ]; then
161	  pass "Delete directories"
162	else
163	  fail "Delete directories (e2e-dest-folder exists: $(test -d "$MP/e2e-dest-folder" && echo yes || echo no), e2e-folder-renamed exists: $(test -d "$MP/e2e-folder-renamed" && echo yes || echo no))"
164	fi
165	
166	# ---- Test 9: Cleanup ----
167	echo "--- Test 9: Cleanup ---"
168	rm -f "$MP/e2e-binary.bin" 2>/dev/null || true
169	rm -f /tmp/e2e-binary.bin 2>/dev/null || true
170	# Filter .DS_Store from any remaining file checks
171	REMAINING=$(ls "$MP" 2>/dev/null | grep -v '\.DS_Store' | grep -v '^$' || true)
172	if [ -z "$REMAINING" ]; then
173	  pass "Cleanup (mount point clean)"
174	else
175	  # Not a hard failure -- other files may be present from the desktop app
176	  pass "Cleanup (remaining files: $REMAINING)"
177	fi
178	
179	# ---- Summary ----
180	echo ""
181	echo "=== FUSE File I/O Results ==="
182	echo "  Passed: $PASS"
183	echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
184	echo "=============================="
185	
186	exit "$FAIL"
187
